==============================================================================
H_RING DEEPENING  (D3)  exclusion theorem in H_ring language
==============================================================================
Each EXCLUDED class forces a used ring with population >= 4:
  rot4/rct4/full : 90-deg rotation -> 4-orbit on one ring (pop 4)
  dia2/ort2      : 2 reflections  -> 4-orbit on one ring (pop 4)
=> that ring contains a 3-subset => S not H_ring-independent
=> NOT missing-center.  (Re-derives the 5-class exclusion theorem.)
